FAQs
SignalIQ is a bank statement analyser that can read unstructured UPI narrations that comprise of 80% of the modern Indian bank statement. It converts transaction narratives into structured credit insights. Most underwriting decisions today are made on partial data, because a large part of financial behaviour sits in unstructured UPI transactions which legacy parsers cannot read. SignalIQ closes that gap by decoding inflows and outflows to give you a complete, decision-ready view of income, obligations, and risk.
